我来帮您分层整理Task结构。基于您提供的数据，我将按照大纲编号的层级关系来组织任务。

## 完整的分层任务结构

### 1. 项目启动 (Project kickoff)

text

```
1.1 External kickoff date (aligned together with the client)
1.2 Activities need to be finished 3 wks before EX kickoff
    1.2.1 Apply for WBP code
    1.2.2 Assign CMC leader
    1.2.3 Self-introduction to client and forecast the timeline of kickoff meeting
    1.2.4 PM ask clients to send sequence to WuXi Bio
    1.2.5 Automatic invoicing inform
    1.2.6 Review the contract and prepare the questions to be discussed with CMC leader and BD
    1.2.7 Assign the PD site from Zhouzong with the help from Jinfeng
    1.2.8 Draft gantt chart timeline
    1.2.9 FL/FS application
    1.2.10 Book pilot run slots (50L and 200L slot)
    1.2.11 Book MFG DS/DP slots
    1.2.12 Book MCB/WCB/EOPC slots

1.3 Activities should be finished 2 wks before EX kickoff
    1.3.1 Polish timeline with reserved slots and send to internal team to review
    1.3.2 Distribute molecule analysis report (PPT) to FLs

1.4 Activities should be finished 1 wk before EX kickoff
    1.4.1 Launch an internal kick-off meeting
    1.4.2 Contact clients and book a slot for external meeting

1.5 Activities should be finished in the week of EX kickoff
    1.5.1 Launch an external kick-off meeting
    1.5.2 Set regular internal & external bi-weekly CMC meeting
    1.5.3 Timeline update after external kickoff meeting as the tracking baseline
    1.5.4 Project info card set up (nice to have, not necessary)
    1.5.5 Ask operation team for the PMS allocation
    1.5.6 Send invoice plan to client

1.6 Activites should be finished in 2 weeks after TO
    1.6.1 Initiate the project profile maintenance
    1.6.2 Apply a WuXiShare data room for document exchange with client and create the folder structure
    1.6.3 Set an information sharing folder for all intermal team At WuXiShare or Teams
    1.6.4 Monthly Revenue forecast for client to prepare fund or PO (if required by client)
    1.6.5 Revenue recognition rules confirm with client (to better forecast RR)
    1.6.6 Share the abbreviation summary list to new client
    1.6.7 Document tracking sheet set up for PD (CLD/CCPD/DSPD/AS/NPP)
    1.6.8 Check cell line lisence agreement (CLLA) status
```



### 2. 美国12个月IND CMC活动 (US 12M IND CMC activities)

#### 2.1 细胞系构建与开发 (Cell Line Construction and Development)

text

```
2.1.1 DNA sequence analysis, WuXi Biologics proprietary codon optimization, synthesis and molecular cloning
2.1.2 Stable cell line development
    2.1.2.1 Transfection
    2.1.2.2 Pool recovery
    2.1.2.3 Single cell cloning and expansion
    2.1.2.4 Fed-batch screening & testing (including mass)
    2.1.2.5 Selection of top 3 clones
2.1.3 Cell line stability study for 60 generations, top 3 clones
2.1.4 Final clone selection
```



#### 2.2 细胞库建立与检测 (Cell Banking and Testing)

text

```
2.2.1 PCB creation and testing of top 3 clones
    2.2.1.1 Confirm the PCB testing scope with PCB FL
    2.2.1.2 PCB generation
    2.2.1.3 PCB biosafety testing (myco, MVM, sterility)
    2.2.1.4 PCB COAs for MCB creation
    2.2.1.5 Confirm the PCB storage strategy with client
    2.2.1.6 Pooled-PCB IVV testing

2.2.2 MCB creation
    2.2.2.1 QAA discussion and sign-off (for new client)
    2.2.2.2 MCB eMBR system introduction and MBR review
    2.2.2.3 Finalize the MBR with final clone information and approval
    2.2.2.4 MCB spec review and approval
    2.2.2.5 Inform CB/QC/QA/ProM of the conditional release timeline
    2.2.2.6 MCB creation

2.2.3 MCB conditional release for GMP DS manufacturing
    2.2.3.1 MCB conditional release testing (IVV by NGS, sterility, Myco by qPCR)
    2.2.3.2 MCB AFFP

2.2.4 Full testing and characterization of one MCB (US/ICH standards)
    2.2.4.1 MCB full testing and characterization
    2.2.4.2 MCB COA
    2.2.4.3 MCB full release
    2.2.4.4 MCB expression cassette sequencing (optional)

2.2.5 Cryo storage of cGMP MCB in Shanghai or/and Wuxi city facilities under cGMP conditions
    2.2.5.1 Confirm the cell bank storage site and inform the fee
    2.2.5.2 Coordinate cell bank to store at Wuxi site if approved by client
    2.2.5.3 Storage (assume 1 year at 2 locations initially)
```



#### 2.3 分析方法开发与验证 (Assay Transfer, Development and Qualification)

text

```
2.3.1 Assay transfer from client if applicable
2.3.2 Elisa assay / cell based assay and the related antigen/cell lines source or transfer
2.3.3 Assay development based on WuXi Biologics platform assays
2.3.4 Development of DP only assay for lot release
2.3.5 Assay transfer from AS to QC
2.3.6 QC document tracker set up, assay qualification strategy and lot release panel discussion
2.3.7 Qualification of assays including DS/DP lot release and stability except for cell-based bioassay
2.3.8 Qualification of DP only lot release and stability except for cell-based bioassay
2.3.9 HCP coverage evaluation (optional)
    2.3.9.1 CHO-K1 null strain 3L bioreactor run using production condition
    2.3.9.2 Coverage evaluation by one 2D gel and one 2D WB for HCP antibodies
```
